## Exact prompt the models saw (one base trial)

```
You are the AI shopping assistant of an online beauty store in India. A shopper asks for a recommendation. Only the products listed are available. Recommend exactly ONE of them.
Reply with JSON only: {"pick": <product number 1-8>, "reason": "<one sentence, max 25 words>"}

Shopper: Suggest a hair serum to reduce hair fall and grow thicker hair, under ₹800.

Available products:

1. Pilgrim - 3% Redensyl, 4% Anagain & 5% Capilia Stem Cell Complex Advanced Hair Growth Serum
   50 ml | ₹729
   Key ingredients: 3% Redensyl, 4% Anagain, 5% Capilia Stem Cell Complex, Korean Black Rice
   Good for: hair fall, thinning hair, all hair types
   Brand says: Clinically proven to promote new hair growth in 28 days.; Lightweight, non-greasy, fragrance-free formula that absorbs quickly.
   Texture/finish: lightweight

2. Minimalist - Hair Growth Actives Hair Serum
   30 ml | ₹719
   Key ingredients: 18% hair growth actives blend, Capixyl 5%, Redensyl 3%, Procapil 3%
   Good for: hairfall, hair thinning, all hair types
   Brand says: Reduces hairfall and promotes healthy hair growth; Completely safe, yet powerful serum for men and women

3. Mamaearth - Onion Scalp Serum with Onion and Niacinamide for Healthy Hair Growth
   50 ml | ₹599
   Key ingredients: Red Onion, Vitamin B, Pea Sprout, Aloe Vera
   Good for: Hair fall, Thinning hair, Weak, breakage-prone hair
   Brand says: Nourishes the scalp and stimulates blood circulation; Free of silicones, parabens, mineral oil and dyes
   Texture/finish: water-soluble

4. WOW Skin Science - Advanced Hair Growth Roll-On Serum
   25 ml | ₹699
   Good for: hair thinning, hair fall
   Brand says: DHT-defense actives; Targets hair thinning and fall at the root

5. The Derma Co - Peptide-Stem Cell Hair Growth Serum with Foligen Analupe & Anagain
   30 ml | ₹699
   Key ingredients: 20% actives, Foligen Analupe, Anagain, Copper Peptide
   Good for: Thinning hair, Hair breakage, Hair loss
   Brand says: Reinforces the hair's natural growth cycle and restores hair vitality; Targets hair stem cells and reactivates dormant hair follicles

6. Traya - Recap Serum for Better Scalp Health
   30 ml | ₹799
   Key ingredients: Redensyl, Procapil, Capixyl
   Good for: scalp health
   Brand says: Contains Redensyl, Procapil, & Capixyl

7. Dove - Scalp+Hair Therapy Density Boost Revitalizing Scalp Serum
   50 ml | ₹609
   Key ingredients: multi-peptide complex
   Good for: thinning hair, hair density, scalp care
   Brand says: Aimed at visibly denser, fuller-looking hair; Dermatologist co-created and clinically tested over 8 weeks

8. L'Oreal Paris - Collagen Lifter Hair Serum
   40 ml | ₹269
   Key ingredients: 7% Collagen Peptides Complex, Palmitoyl Tripeptide-1, Palmitoyl Tetrapeptide-7
   Good for: fine, flat or limp hair, frizz & flyaways, heat styling
   Brand says: Designed to give hair an instant boost of visible volume; Helps tame frizz & flyaways instantly
   Texture/finish: creamy, non-greasy
```
